 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '04bd3290-f32b-4544-9873-5deebfc4e1ab-1774626946' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{56A6F049}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1698 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='04bd3290-f32b-4544-9873-5deebfc4e1ab-1774626946' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='04bd3290-f32b-4544-9873-5deebfc4e1ab-1774626946' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '04bd3290-f32b-4544-9873-5deebfc4e1ab-1774626946' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '04bd3290-f32b-4544-9873-5deebfc4e1ab-1774626946' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{56A6F049}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1698 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='04bd3290-f32b-4544-9873-5deebfc4e1ab-1774626946' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='04bd3290-f32b-4544-9873-5deebfc4e1ab-1774626946' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '04bd3290-f32b-4544-9873-5deebfc4e1ab-1774626946' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='0e21c1ae-7fb1-45b9-8b1d-76065c364d41-1761768998' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '0e21c1ae-7fb1-45b9-8b1d-76065c364d41-1761768998' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'bffae9a7-8729-495a-a951-0b0f6529b2cc-1773249073' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{61D0609A}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1619 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='bffae9a7-8729-495a-a951-0b0f6529b2cc-1773249073' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='bffae9a7-8729-495a-a951-0b0f6529b2cc-1773249073' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = 'bffae9a7-8729-495a-a951-0b0f6529b2cc-1773249073' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1699 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='e8ba1ca2-e5fb-4c0e-a6bd-08cfc732e0d4-1774631303' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc

select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='93540390-cc55-4755-bab1-2a621a95e6a4-1774634148' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='93540390-cc55-4755-bab1-2a621a95e6a4-1774634148' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '93540390-cc55-4755-bab1-2a621a95e6a4-1774634148' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'a23fd9c1-813e-442e-a73b-7f4c17aba9f6-1774644488' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{39A8E23B}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1703 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='a23fd9c1-813e-442e-a73b-7f4c17aba9f6-1774644488' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='a23fd9c1-813e-442e-a73b-7f4c17aba9f6-1774644488' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc

select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='2924abd0-1b45-45ac-89db-cd7925fa1806-1760038094' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='2924abd0-1b45-45ac-89db-cd7925fa1806-1760038094' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = '2924abd0-1b45-45ac-89db-cd7925fa1806-1760038094' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{396F637D}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=81 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='2924abd0-1b45-45ac-89db-cd7925fa1806-1760038094' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='2924abd0-1b45-45ac-89db-cd7925fa1806-1760038094' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
SELECT ecp.play_count_allowed_credit AS `Credit Allowed`, IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0) AS `Credit Used`, (ecp.play_count_allowed_credit - IFNULL(ecp.used_credit, 0)) AS `Credit Balance`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.start_date_time - 19800) AS `Start Date`, FROM_UNIXTIME(ecp.end_date_time - 19800) AS `End Date`, ep.name AS `Package Name`, ep.price AS `Package Price` from es_customer AS ec INNER JOIN es_customer_package AS ecp ON ec.id= ecp.customer_id  INNER JOIN es_package AS ep ON ep.id = ecp.package_id where ec.guid = '2924abd0-1b45-45ac-89db-cd7925fa1806-1760038094' ORDER BY ecp.end_date_time DESC;

select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1704 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='ad6a04c8-f5c2-4902-a9ce-950fe6e8329d-1774647527' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='ad6a04c8-f5c2-4902-a9ce-950fe6e8329d-1774647527' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc

 SELECT  	eslt.*, esg.id as game_id, esg.name AS game_name, ec.name AS customer_name, ec.phone AS customer_phone  FROM  	es_softplay_live_trans AS  eslt INNER JOIN  	es_customer AS ec ON ec.rfid = eslt.customer_rfid AND ec.guid = eslt.customer_guid INNER JOIN  	es_softplay_games AS esg ON esg.id = eslt.softplay_game_id  WHERE  	eslt.is_reconciled = 1 AND  	eslt.punch_out_unix_timestamp > 0 and	 	eslt.id IN (SELECT MAX(n_eslt.id) FROM es_softplay_live_trans AS n_eslt WHERE n_eslt.customer_guid = 'ad6a04c8-f5c2-4902-a9ce-950fe6e8329d-1774647527' AND n_eslt.customer_rfid = '{39AA0AD1}' GROUP BY n_eslt.es_softplay_live_id, n_eslt.customer_guid, n_eslt.customer_rfid)  order by eslt.es_softplay_live_id DESC, eslt.id DESC
select _eb.id as `Billing Id`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.log_device_ip `Device IP`, _eb.log_game `Game`, _eb.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _eb.amount as `Payed Amount`, _eb.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _eb.token_earn as `Ticket Earn`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.is_refund as `Is Refund` from es_billing as _eb where log_game<>'' and _eb.customer_id=1704 and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
select _ep.id as `Payment ID`, _ep.rfid as `RFID`, GUID, _ep.name as `Name`, _ep.phone as `Phone`, _ep.payment_mode as `Payment Mode`, _ep.pre_amount as `Pre Amount`, _ep.recharge_amount as `Recharge Amount`, _ep.discount_amount_on_bill as `Discount Amount`, (_ep.recharge_amount - _ep.discount_amount_on_bill) as `Collect Amount`, ROUND( _ep.collect_amount_from_customer - _ep.gst_amount ,2 ) as `Taxable Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `CGST Amount`, ROUND(_ep.gst_amount / 2 ,  2) as `SGST Amount`, _ep.main_amount as `Main Amount`, _ep.bonus_amount as `Bonus Amount`, _ep.post_amount as `Post Amount`, _ep.user as `User`, _ep.created_at as `Date` from es_payment as _ep where _ep.guid='ad6a04c8-f5c2-4902-a9ce-950fe6e8329d-1774647527' and _ep.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _ep.id desc
select _eb.id as `Request Id`, (Case WHEN _eb.is_reedem = 1 THEN 'Redeem' Else 'Added' End) As `Is Reedem`, _eb.rfid as `RFID`, _eb.GUID, _eb.name as `Name`, _eb.phone as `Phone`, _eb.redeem_gift `Gift`, _eb.pre_ticket as `Pre Ticket`, _eb.ticket as `Amount`, _eb.post_ticket as `Post Ticket`, _eb.created_at as `Date`, _eb.updated_by as `User` from es_token_log as _eb where _eb.guid='ad6a04c8-f5c2-4902-a9ce-950fe6e8329d-1774647527' and _eb.is_deleted <> 'yes' order by _eb.id desc
